class On(BDDLPredicate):

```

def _on(obj1, obj2, ...):

外部DeepSeek原记录 thinking
pi0_pick reports success: gripper closing 0.034 (holding something of ~3.4cm width), lift 0.05, eef at (0.070, 0.088, 0.554). The agentview shows the mug still hanging/grasped? In 0923de the mug appears... the fingers are gripping the mug's handle region at the top-left of the mug. The mug's orientation looks like it's hanging with the axis still horizontal-ish (base toward the camera-left, opening at the right).

So the mug is now lifted slightly, held by the handle, still lying orientation (axis horizontal). Gripper closing 0.034 → the fingers are ~3.4cm apart, gripping the handle tube presumably.

Hmm. The mug held by the handle with the axis horizontal. If I now lift it fully and then rotate the wrist 90° (pitch), the mug's axis would... The handle grip: the finger closing direction is along world y (yaw=0 with pitch ~9.5°). The mug's axis is roughly (+x,−y) horizontal. Rotating about world x by 90° would turn the y component into z. The axis (+x,−y) → (+x, 0, −(−1)) hmm: rotating (0,−1,0) by +90° about x gives (0, 0, −1)... Let's compute: R_x(90°): y→z, z→−y. So (0,−1,0) → (0,0,−1): the −y component becomes −z. And the +x component stays +x. So the mug axis becomes (+0.7x, 0, −0.7z) — i.e., tilted 45° from vertical. Not upright, but tilted. Rotating by ~135° would make the y-component vertical but leave the x component... The x component can't be fixed by pitch.

Hmm. So the mug's axis has an x-component that pitch can't remove. But yaw can rotate the x-component into y! Procedure:
1. Firm the grip (set_gripper +1).
2. rotate_wrist by some delta_yaw to align the mug's axis exactly along world −y (or +y). We know the axis is roughly (+x,−y) at ~45°. Yaw rotation changes the axis direction in the xy-plane. To put the axis along −y: rotate yaw by... current axis direction angle θ_a = atan2(ay, ax) = atan2(−0.7, 0.7) = −45°. Target: along ±y = angle −90° or +90°. So delta yaw = −45° (i.e., −0.785 rad) puts the axis along −y. (Yaw rotation by δ adds δ to the vector's azimuth angle.)
3. Then rotate_pitch by +90° or −90° to bring the −y axis to vertical: R_x(+90): (0,−1,0) → (0,0,−1) → the mug's "opening" ends up pointing DOWN → upside-down mug. Use R_x(−90): (0,−1,0) → (0,0,+1) → the opening points UP → upright mug.

```

So my assumption \"the axis is along x, length 0.10\" may be wrong, or the mug's center is farther −x than 0.052.\n\nLet's recheck with the wrist image (5312e6, eef at (0.0, 0.055)):\n- The mug's silhouette: cols ~320-640, rows ~485-870 (clipped).
